## ०१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/21 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[अथ|अ᳓थ]] स्वयमातृण्णा᳓सु सा᳓मानि गायतीमे᳓ वै᳓ लोकाः᳓ स्वयमातृण्णा᳓स्ता᳓ एताः᳓ [[शर्करास्ता᳓|श᳓र्करास्ता᳓]] देवा᳓ [[उपधा᳓यैतादृ᳓शीरेवा᳓पश्यन्यथैताः᳓|उपधा᳓यैतादृ᳓शीरेवा᳓पश्यन्य᳓थैताः᳓]] शु᳓ष्काः श᳓र्कराः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

On the naturally perforated bricks, he sings Sāmans, for, the svayamātṛṇṇā are these (three) worlds and they are (represented by) three stones. After placing (once upon a time) saw them as such, dry stones.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अथ स्वयमातृण्णा᳘सु सा᳘मानि गायतीमे वै᳘ लोकाः᳘ स्वयमातृण्णास्ता᳘ एताः शर्करास्ता᳘ देवा᳘ उपधा᳘यै तादृ᳘शीरेवापश्यन्यथैताः शु᳘ष्काः श᳘र्कराः᳘ ॥१॥
</details>

## ०२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/31 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

ते᳙ऽब्रुवन्नु᳓प त᳓ज्जानीत य᳓थैषु᳓ लोके᳓षु र᳓समुपजी᳓वनं दधामे᳓ति [[तेऽब्रुवंश्चेत᳓यध्वमिति|ते᳙ऽब्रुवंश्चेत᳓यध्वमि᳓ति]] चिति᳓मिच्छ᳓ते᳓ति वाव᳓ त᳓दब्रुवंस्त᳓दिच्छत य᳓थैषु᳓ लोके᳓षु र᳓समुपजी᳓वनं दधामे᳓ति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

They said (to themselves), “Think as to how we can put essence, the nourishing ingredient, into these worlds.” They said, “Meditate” by which they meant “wish for a layer.” So they wished as to how the essence, the nourishing ingredient can be placed into these worlds.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तेऽब्रुवन्नु᳘प त᳘ज्जानीत य᳘थैषु᳘ लोके᳘षु र᳘समुपजी᳘वनं दधामे᳘ति तेऽब्रुवꣳश्चेत᳘यध्वमिति चिति᳘मिच्छते᳘ति वाव त᳘दब्रुवꣳस्त᳘दिच्छत य᳘थैषु᳘ लोके᳘षु र᳘समुपजी᳘वनमं दधामे᳘ति ॥२॥
</details>

## ०३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ते᳓ चेत᳓यमाना एता᳓नि सा᳓मान्यपश्यं स्ता᳓न्यगायंस्तै᳓रेषु लोके᳓षु र᳓समुपजी᳓वनमदधुः ।
</details>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

3. Whilst meditating, they saw these sāmans (hymn-tunes), and sang them; and by means of them they laid sap, the means of subsistence, into these worlds;
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

While meditating, they discovered these Sāman hymns. They sang them.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

ते᳘ चेत᳘यमानाः॥

</details>

## ०४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स्वयमातृण्णा᳓सु गायतीमे᳓ वै᳓ लोकाः᳓ स्वयमातृण्णा᳓ एष्वे᳙वै᳙त᳓ल्लोके᳓षु र᳓समुपजी᳓वनं दधाति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/08/07/4/08_svayamAtRNNAsu_gAyati_ime.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

4. Over the naturally-perforated ones he sings them: the naturally-perforated ones being these worlds, it is into these worlds that he thereby lays sap, the means of subsistence.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He sings over the naturally perforated bricks. Svayamātṛṇṇā are these worlds and thereby he puts essence, the nourishing ingredient into these worlds.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

स्वयमातृण्णा᳘सु गायति॥  
(ती) इमे वै᳘ लोकाः᳘ स्वयमातृण्णा᳘ ऽए᳘ष्वे᳘वैत᳘ल्लोके᳘षु र᳘समुपजी᳘वनं दधाति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

स्वयमातृणा᳘सु गायति॥  
इमे वै᳘ लोकाः᳘ स्वयमातृणा᳘ एॗष्वेॗवैत᳘ल्लोके᳘षु र᳘समुपजी᳘वनं दधाति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

स्वयमातृण्णासु गायति । इमे वै लोकाः स्वयमातृण्णाः । एष्वेवैतल्लोकेषु रसमुपजीवनं दधाति ॥ ४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

स्वयमातृण्णा᳘सु गायतीमे वै᳘ लोकाः᳘ स्वयमातृण्णा᳘ ए᳘ष्वे᳘वैत᳘ल्लोके᳘षु र᳘समुपजी᳘वनं दधाति ॥४॥
</details>

--bhūs (earth), doubtless, is this world, bhuvas is the air-world, and svar (light) is yonder world: into these worlds he thereby lays sap, the means of subsistence.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Bhūḥ stands for this world (earth). Bhuvaḥ stands for the aerial region and Svaḥ stands for the yonder (heaven).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

</details>

</details>

## ०६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/10 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

ता᳓नि वै᳓ ना᳓नाप्रस्तावानि समान᳓निधनानि ता᳓नि य᳓न्ना᳓नाप्रस्तावानि [[नाना|ना᳓ना]] ह्य᳓पश्यन् ।
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/08/07/4/12_tAni_vai_nAnAprastAvAni.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

6. They have different preludes, and the same finale [^egg_255]; and as to their having different preludes, it

is because they (the gods) saw them separately;

[^egg_255]: 145:1 These hymn-tunes are given, Sam. Ved. V, p. 487, in the way in which they are here to be chanted. They consist entirely of the respective words, separated four times by musical interjections (stobhas) inserted between them, ending with the common finale: thus, (r) bhūḥ--bhūḥ--hoyi--bhūḥ--hoyi--bhūḥ--hā~ūvā~e--suvarjyotī~ḥ; (2) bhuvāḥ--bhuvaḥ--hoyi--bhuvaḥ--hā~ūvā~e--suvarjyotī~ḥ; (3) suvāḥ--suvaḥ--hoyi--suvaḥ--hoyi--suvaḥ--hā~ūvā~e--suvarjyotī~ḥ. Along with these, as to be chanted on the same model, are given, (4) the 'satyaṁ sāman,' beginning 'satyām--satyam--hoyi' &c., and  (5) the 'purusha-sāman,' beginning 'purushāḥ--purusha--hoyi' &c. which are similarly chanted by the Sacrificer at the beginning of the first layer, when laying down the lotus leaf (part iii, p. 363, where note 1 should be corrected in accordance with the present note), and the gold man (ib. p. 369, where the note requires likewise to be corrected), as the 'citre gāyati,' 'he sings on the bright one,' of the text cannot refer to the 'Citra-sāman' there referred to. Cf. Lāṭy. S. I, 5, 8.--In regard to these sāmans (hymn-verses), the text might lead one to suppose that they only consist of two, instead of the usual four parts (omitting the intermediate Udgītha and Pratihāra, cf. part ii, p. 310 note). The sāman being, however, sung by the Sacrificer himself, the usual distinction into parts to be performed by different chanters was probably dispensed with.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The Sāmans have different prastāvas (startings) but have the same nidhana (ending). They have different startings because the gods discov- ered them separately.
</details>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/15 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓त्समान᳓निधनान्ये᳓का ह्ये᳙व᳓ यज्ञ᳓स्य प्रतिष्ठै᳓कं [[निधनं|निध᳓नं]] स्वर्ग᳓ एव᳓ [[लोक᳓स्तस्मात्स्व-र्ज्योतीर्निधनानि|लोक᳓स्त᳓स्मात्स्व᳓-र्ज्योतीर्निधनानि]] ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/08/07/4/13_atha_yatsamAnanidhanAnyekA_hyeva.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

and as to their having the same finale (nidhana), it is because there is only one foundation, only one finale to the sacrifice--even heaven: therefore they have 'svar-jyotis (heaven-light)' for their finale.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

As to why they have a common ending is because there is only one culmination (goal) for the sacrifice, i.e., the heaven itself. Therefore, they all end in (the word) svarjyotiḥ (heavenly light).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘त्समान᳘निधनान्ये᳘का᳘ ह्येव᳘ यज्ञ᳘स्य प्रतिष्ठै᳘कं निध᳘नᳫँ᳭ स्वर्ग᳘ एव᳘ लोकस्त᳘स्मा᳘त्स्वर्ज्योतिर्निधनानि[[!!]]॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘त्समान᳘निधनान्ये᳘काॗ ह्येव᳘ यज्ञ᳘स्य प्रतिष्ठै᳘कं निध᳘नᳫं स्वर्ग᳘ एव᳘ लोकस्त᳘स्मात्स्व᳘र्ज्योतिर्निधनानि॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

अथ यत्समाननिधनानि- एका ह्येव यज्ञस्य प्रतिष्ठा, एकं निधनं स्वर्ग एव लोकः । तस्मात्स्वर्ज्योतिर्निधनानि ॥ ६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/17 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थैनं हिरण्यशकलैः᳓ प्रो᳓क्षत्य᳓त्रैष᳓ [[सर्वोऽग्निः᳓|स᳓र्वोऽग्निः᳓]] [[सं᳓स्कृतस्त᳓स्मिन्देवा|सं᳓स्कृतस्त᳓स्मिन्देवा᳓]] एत᳓दमृ᳓तं [[रूपमुत्तम᳓मदधुः|रूप᳓मुत्तम᳓मदधुः]] ।
</details>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/19 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

द्वा᳓भ्यां द्वाभ्यां द्विपा᳓द्य᳓जमानो य᳓जमानो᳓ऽग्निर्या᳓वानग्नि᳓र्या᳓वत्यस्य मात्रा᳓ [[तावतैवा᳓स्मिन्नेतदमृतं|ता᳓वतैवा᳓स्मिन्नेत᳓दमृ᳓तं]] [[रूपमुत्तमं|रूप᳓मुत्तमं᳓]] दधाति ।
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/08/07/4/19_dvAbhyAM_dvAbhyAM_shatAbhyAm.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

9. With two hundred (chips he bestrews him) each time,--two-footed is the Sacrificer, and Agni

is the sacrificer: as great as Agni is, as great as is his measure, with so much he thus bestows upon him immortality, that highest form.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

With two hundred pieces for each go, (he decorates him); for, the Yajamāna is two-footed and the Yajamāna is Agni-altar; as great as Agni is, as great as is his size, with so much he thus confers on him that supreme form of immortality.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

द्वा᳘भ्यां द्वाभ्याᳫँ᳭ शता᳘भ्याम्॥  
(न्द्वि) द्विपाद्य᳘जमानो य᳘जमानो ऽग्निर्या᳘वानग्निर्या᳘वत्यस्य मा᳘त्रा ता᳘वतै᳘वास्मिन्नेत᳘दमृ᳘तᳫँ᳭ रूप᳘मुत्तमं᳘ दधाति
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

द्वा᳘भ्यां-द्वाभ्यां शता᳘भ्याम्॥  
द्विपाद्य᳘जमानो य᳘जमानो ऽग्निर्या᳘वानग्निर्या᳘वत्यस्य मा᳘त्रा ता᳘वतैॗवास्मिन्नेत᳘दमृ᳘तं रूप᳘मुत्तमं᳘ दधाति
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

द्वाभ्यां द्वाभ्यां शताभ्याम् । द्विपाद्यजमानः । यजमानो ऽग्निः । यावानग्निर्यावत्यस्य मात्रा- तावतैवास्मिन्नेतदमृतं रूपमुत्तमं दधाति ।
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

द्वा᳘भ्यां द्वाभ्याꣳ द्विपाद्य᳘जमानो य᳘जमानो᳘ऽग्निर्या᳘वानग्निर्या᳘वत्यस्य मात्रा तावतैवास्मिन्नेतदमृतꣳ रुपमुत्तमं दधाति

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/22 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[प᳓ञ्चकृत्वः|प᳓ञ्चकृ᳓त्वः]] प᳓ञ्चचितिकोऽग्निः᳓ प᳓ञ्चर्त᳓वः [[संवत्सरः|संवत्सरः᳓]] संवत्सरो᳙ऽग्नि᳓र्या᳓वानग्नि᳓र्या᳓वत्यस्य मा᳓त्रा [[तावतैवा᳙स्मिन्नेतदमृतं|ता᳓वतैवा᳙स्मिन्नेत᳓दमृ᳓तं]] रूप᳓मुत्तमं᳓ दधाति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Five times he thus strews (gold pieces) be- cause Agni-altar has five layers and there are five seasons for the year and the year is Agni-altar; as great as Agni is, as great as is his size, with so much he thus confers that supreme form of immortality on him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

प᳘ञ्चकृत्वः प᳘ञ्चचितिकोऽग्निः प᳘ञ्चर्त᳘वः संवत्सरः संवत्स᳘रोऽग्निर्या᳘वानग्निर्या᳘वत्यस्य मा᳘त्रा तावतै᳘वास्मिन्नै᳘तदमृतꣳ रूप᳘मुत्तमुं᳘ दधाति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/36 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

पश्चा᳓दग्नेः᳓ प्रा᳓ङ्ति᳓ष्ठन्न᳓थोत्तरतो᳓ दक्षिणा᳓थ पुर᳓स्तात्प्रत्य᳓ङ᳓थ जघ᳓नेन परी᳓त्य दक्षिणत᳓ उ᳓दङ्ति᳓ष्ठंस्त᳓द्दक्षिणावृ᳓त्त᳓द्धि᳓ [[देवत्राथानु|देवत्रा᳓था᳓नु]] [[परीत्य|परी᳓त्य]] पश्चा᳓त्प्रा᳓ङ्ति᳓ष्ठंस्त᳓थो हास्यै᳓त᳓त्प्रा᳓गेव᳓ क᳓र्म कृतं᳓ भवति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/08/07/4/23_pashchAdagre_prA~N_tiShThan.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

10. First (he scatters them) at the back whilst standing with his face towards the east; then on the left (north) side towards the south; then in front whilst facing the west; then, having gone round the back, from the south whilst facing the north: this is from left to right (sunwise), for that is (the way) with the gods. Then, having gone round, (he scatters chips) at the back whilst standing with his face to the east, for in this way that former performance of him [^egg_258] took place.

[^egg_258]: 147:1 Viz. of Agni (and the Sacrificer). The ceremony alluded to was the fivefold libation of ghee offered on the gold man (representing Agni and the Sacrificer), see VII, 4, 1, 34-35.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

then on the left (north side) facing the south; then in the front (east) facing the west; then having gone round the back from the south while facing the north. This is from left to right for, that is the course of the gods. Then, having gone round, (he scatters) at the back standing east-faced. This is the order and in which he did earlier also (while offering ghee on the golden human figure).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

पश्चाद᳘ग्रे प्राङ्ति᳘ष्ठन्॥  
(न्न᳘) अ᳘थोत्तरतो᳘ दक्षिणा᳘ ऽथ पुर᳘स्तात्प्रत्यङ्ङ᳘थ जघ᳘नेन परी᳘त्य दक्षिणत ऽउ᳘दङ्ति᳘ष्ठंस्त᳘द्दक्षिणावृत्तद्धि᳘ देवत्रा᳘ ऽथानुपरी᳘त्य पश्चात्प्राङ्ति᳘ष्ठंस्त᳘थो हास्यैतत्प्रा᳘गेव क᳘र्म कृतं᳘ भवति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

पश्चाद᳘ग्रे प्राङ् ति᳘ष्ठन्॥  
अ᳘थोत्तरतो᳘ दक्षिणा᳘थ पुर᳘स्तात्प्रत्यङ्ङ᳘थ जघ᳘नेन परी᳘त्य दक्षिणत उ᳘दङ् ति᳘ष्ठंस्त᳘द्दक्षिणावृत्तद्धि᳘ देवत्रा᳘थानुपरी᳘त्य पश्चात्प्राङ् ति᳘ष्ठंस्त᳘थो हास्यैतत्प्रा᳘गेव क᳘र्म कृत᳘म् भवति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

पश्चादग्रे प्राङ् तिष्ठन् । अथोत्तरतो दक्षिणा । अथ पुरस्तात्प्रत्यङ् । अथ जघनेन परीत्य दक्षिणत उदङ् तिष्ठन् । तद्दक्षिणावृत् । तद्धि देवत्रा । अथानुपरीत्य पश्चात्प्राङ् तिष्ठन् । तथो हास्यैतत्प्रागेव कर्म कृतं भवति ॥ १० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

पश्चा᳘दग्नेः प्राङ्ति᳘ष्ठन्न᳘थोत्तरतो᳘ दक्षिणा᳘थ पुर᳘स्तात्प्रत्यङ᳘थ जघ᳘नेन परी᳘त्य दक्षिणत उ᳘दङ्ति᳘ष्ठꣳस्त᳘द्दक्षिणावृत्तद्धि᳘ देवत्रा᳘थानु परीत्य पश्चात्प्राङ्ति᳘ष्ठꣳस्त᳘थो हास्यैतत्प्रा᳘गेव क᳘र्म कृतं᳘ भवति ॥८॥
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/14 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

सह᳓स्रस्य प्र᳓मासि सह᳓स्रस्य [[प्रति᳓मासि|प्र᳓तिमासि]] सह᳓स्रस्यो᳓न्मासि साहस्रो᳙ऽसि सह᳓स्राय त्वे᳓ति ।

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/08/07/4/24_sahasrasya_pramAsi_sahasrasya.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

11. [He scatters, with, Vāj. S. XV, 65], 'The fore-measure of a thousand thou art,--The counter-measure of a thousand thou art,--The up-measure of a thousand thou art,--The thousandfold thou art, --For a thousand thee!'
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

(While scattering, he says) "You are the measure of a thousand; you are the counter-measure of a thousand, you are the measure of altitude of a thousand; you are a thousandfold and to you, thousand"—for, the thou- sand means "all".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

सह᳘स्रस्य प्र᳘मा ऽसि॥  
सह᳘स्रस्य प्रति᳘मा ऽसि सह᳘स्रस्यो᳘न्मा ऽसि साह᳘स्रो ऽसि सह᳘स्राय त्वे᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

सह᳘स्रस्य प्रॗमासि॥  
सह᳘स्रस्य प्रतिॗमासि सह᳘स्रस्योॗन्मासि साहॗस्रो ऽसि सह᳘स्राय त्वे᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

**"सहस्रस्य प्रमा ऽसि, सहस्रस्य प्रतिमा ऽसि, सहस्रस्योन्मा ऽसि, साहस्रो ऽसि, सहस्राय त्वा"**- (वा. सं. १५ । ६५) इति ।
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

सह᳘स्रस्य प्र᳘मासि सह᳘स्रस्य प्रति᳘मासि सह᳘स्रस्यो᳘न्मासि साह᳘स्रोऽसि सह᳘स्राय त्वे᳘ति
</details>

## १०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/24 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓था᳓तश्चितिपुरीषा᳓णामेव᳓ [[मीमां᳓साय᳓मेव᳓|मीमांसा᳓य᳓मेव᳓]] कोकः᳓ प्रथमा᳓ चि᳓तिः पश᳓वः पु᳓रीषं य᳓त्प्रथमां᳓ चि᳓तिं पु᳓रीषेण [[प्र᳓च्छाद᳓यतीमं᳓|प्रच्छाद᳓यतीमं᳓]] त᳓ल्लोकं᳓ पशु᳓भिः प्र᳓च्छादयति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Now follows the discussion on the loose soil of the altar. The first layer is this (terrestrial) world and the loose soil (used for filling) is the cattle. When he fills the first layer with loose soil, he fills it with cattle.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अथा᳘तश्चितिपुरीषा᳘णामेव᳘ मीमा᳘ꣳसाय᳘मेव᳘ कोकः᳘ प्रथमा चि᳘तिः पश᳘वः पु᳘रीषं य᳘त्प्रथमां चि᳘तिं पु᳘रीषेण प्र᳘च्छाद᳘यतीमं᳘ त᳘ल्लोकं᳘ पशु᳘भिः प्र᳘च्छादयति ॥१०॥
</details>

## ११

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/17 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अन्त᳓रिक्षमेव᳓ [[द्वितीया|द्विती᳓या]] चि᳓तिर्व᳓यांसि पु᳓रीषं [[यद्द्विती᳓यां|य᳓द्द्विती᳓यां]] चि᳓तिं पु᳓रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳓यत्यन्त᳓रिक्षं त᳓द्व᳓योभिः प्र᳓च्छादयति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/08/07/4/28_antarixameva_dvitIyA_chitiH.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

13. The second layer is the air, and the filling of soil means birds: thus, in covering the second layer with a filling of soil, he covers (fills) the air with birds.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The second layer is the aerial region and the loose soil (used for filling) are the birds. So when he fills the second layer with loose soil, he fills the aerial region with birds.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य) अन्त᳘रिक्षमेव᳘ द्विती᳘या चि᳘तिः॥  
(र्व्व᳘) व्व᳘याᳫँ᳭सि पु᳘रीषं य᳘द्द्विती᳘यां चि᳘तिं पु᳘रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳘यत्यन्त᳘रिक्षं तद्व᳘योभिः प्र᳘च्छादयति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अन्त᳘रिक्षमेव᳘ द्विती᳘या चि᳘तिः॥  
व᳘यांसि पु᳘रीषं य᳘द्द्विती᳘यां चि᳘तिम् पु᳘रीषेण प्रछाद᳘यत्यन्त᳘रिक्षं तद्व᳘योभिः प्र᳘छादयति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

(२) अन्तरिक्षमेव द्वितीया चितिः । वयांसि पुरीषम् । यद्द्वितीयां चितिं पुरीषेण प्रच्छादयति- अन्तरिक्षं तद्वयोभिः प्रच्छादयति ॥ १३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अन्त᳘रिक्षमेव᳘ द्वितीया चि᳘तिर्व᳘याꣳसि पु᳘रीषं यद्द्विती᳘यां चि᳘तिं पु᳘रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳘यत्यन्त᳘रिक्षं तद्व᳘योभिः प्र᳘च्छादयति ॥११॥
</details>

## १२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/17 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

द्यौ᳓रेव᳓ तृती᳓या चि᳓तिर्न᳓क्षत्राणि पु᳓रीषं य᳓त्तृती᳓यां चि᳓तिं पु᳓रीषेण [[प्र᳓च्छादयति|प्रच्छाद᳓यति]] दि᳓वं त᳓न्न᳓क्षत्रैः प्र᳓च्छादयति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/08/07/4/29_dyaureva_tRtIyA_chitiH.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

14. The third layer is the sky, and the filling of soil means stars; thus, in covering the third layer with a filling of soil, he covers the sky with stars.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The third layer is the sky and the loose soil (used for filling) are the stars. So when he fills the third layer with loose soil, he fills the sky with stars.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

द्यौ᳘रेव᳘ तृती᳘या चि᳘तिः॥  
(र्न᳘) न᳘क्षत्राणि पु᳘रीषं य᳘त्तृती᳘यां चि᳘तिं पु᳘रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳘यति दि᳘वं तन्न᳘क्षत्रैः प्र᳘च्छादयति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

द्यौ᳘रेव᳘ तृती᳘या चि᳘तिः॥  
न᳘क्षत्राणि पु᳘रीषं य᳘त्तृती᳘यां चि᳘तिम् पु᳘रीषेण प्रछाद᳘यति दिवं तन्न᳘क्षत्रैः प्र᳘छादयति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

(३) द्यौरेव तृतीया चितिः । नक्षत्राणि पुरीषम् । यत्तृतीयां चितिं पुरीषेण प्रच्छादयति- दिवं तन्नक्षत्रैः प्रच्छादयति ॥ १४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

द्यौ᳘रेव᳘ तृती᳘या चि᳘तिर्न᳘क्षत्राणि पु᳘रीषं य᳘त्तृती᳘यां चि᳘तिं पु᳘रीषेण प्र᳘च्छादयति दि᳘वं तन्न᳘क्षत्रैः प्र᳘च्छादयति ॥१२॥
</details>

## १३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

यज्ञ᳓ एव᳓ चतुर्थी᳓ चि᳓तिर्द᳓क्षिणाः पु᳓रीषं य᳓ञ्चतुर्थीं᳓ चि᳓तिं पु᳓रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳓यति यज्ञं᳓ त᳓द्द᳓क्षिणाभिः प्र᳓च्छादयति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/08/07/4/30_yajna_eva_chaturthI.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

15. The fourth layer is the sacrifice, and the filling of soil means sacrificial gifts: thus, in covering the fourth layer with a filling of soil, he covers the sacrifice with sacrificial gifts (to the priests).
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The fourth layer is the sacrifice itself and the loose soil (used for filling) are the dakṣiṇā (gifts to the priests) So when he fills the fourth layer with loose soil, he fills the sacrifice with dakṣiṇā.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

यज्ञ᳘ ऽएव᳘ चतुर्थी चि᳘तिः॥  
(र्द᳘) द᳘क्षिणाः पु᳘रीषं य᳘च्चतुर्थीं चि᳘तिं पु᳘रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳘यति यज्ञं तद्द᳘क्षिनाभिः प्र᳘च्छादयति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

यज्ञ᳘ एव᳘ चतुर्थी चि᳘तिः॥  
द᳘क्षिणाः पु᳘रीषं य᳘च्चतुर्थी चि᳘तिम् पु᳘रीषेण प्रछाद᳘यति यज्ञम् तद्द᳘क्षिनाभिः प्र᳘छादयति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

(४) यज्ञ एव चतुर्थी चितिः । दक्षिणाः पुरीषम् । यच्चतुर्थीं चितिं पुरीषेण प्रच्छादयति- यज्ञं तद् दक्षिणाभिः प्रच्छादयति ॥ १५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - काण्व-ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

यज्ञ᳘ एव᳘ चतुर्थी चि᳘तिर्द᳘क्षिणाः पु᳘रीषं य᳘ञ्चतुर्थीं चि᳘तिं पु᳘रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳘यति यज्ञं तद्द᳘क्षिणाभिः प्र᳘च्छादयति ॥१३॥
</details>

## १४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

य᳓जमान एव᳓ पञ्चमी᳓ चि᳓तिः प्रजा᳓ पु᳓रीषं य᳓त्पञ्चमीं᳓ चि᳓तिं पु᳓रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳓यति य᳓जमानं त᳓त्प्रज᳓या प्र᳓च्छादयति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/08/07/4/31_yajamAna_eva_panchamI.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

16. The fifth layer is the Sacrificer, and the filling of soil means progeny (or subjects) thus, in covering the fifth layer with a filling of soil, he covers (abundantly supplies) the Sacrificer with progeny (or subjects).
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The fifth layer is the Yajamāna himself and the loose soil (used for filling) are the offsprings. So when he fills the fifth layer with loose soil, he fills (enriches) the Yajamāna with offsprings.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

य᳘जमान ऽएव᳘ पञ्चमी चि᳘तिः॥  
प्रजा पु᳘रीषं य᳘त्पञ्चमीं चि᳘तिं पु᳘रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳘यति य᳘जमानं त᳘त्प्रज᳘या प्र᳘च्छादयति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

य᳘जमान एव᳘ पञ्चमी चि᳘तिः॥  
प्रजा पु᳘रीषं य᳘त्पञ्चमीं चि᳘तिम् पु᳘रीषेण प्रछाद᳘यति य᳘जमानं त᳘त्प्रज᳘या प्र᳘छादयति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

(५) यजमान एव पञ्चमी चितिः । प्रजा पुरीषम् । यत्पञ्चमीं चितिं पुरीषेण प्रच्छादयति- यजमानं तत् प्रजया प्रच्छादयति ॥ १६ ॥
</details>

## १५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/19 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स्वर्ग᳓ एव᳓ लोकः᳓ षष्ठी᳓ चि᳓तिर्देवाः᳓ पु᳓रीषं य᳓त्षष्ठीं᳓ चि᳓तिं पु᳓रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳓यति स्वर्गं᳓ त᳓ल्लोकं᳓ देवैः᳓ [[प्रच्छादयति|प्र᳓च्छादयति]] ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The sixth layer is the heavenly world and the loose soil (used for filling) are the gods. So when he fills the sixth layer with loose soil, he fills that heavenly world with gods.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स्वर्ग᳘ एव᳘ लोकः᳘ षष्ठी चि᳘तिर्देवाः पु᳘रीषं य᳘त्षष्ठीं चि᳘तिं पु᳘रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳘यति स्वर्गं त᳘ल्लोकं᳘ देवैः प्रच्छादयति ॥१५॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/08/07/4/34_amRtameva_saptamI.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

18. The seventh layer is immortality,--that is the last (layer) he lays down, and thus bestows immortality as the highest thing of all this (universe): therefore immortality is the highest thing of all this (universe);
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The seventh layer is immortality itself. He builds it up last. He thus confers that immortality which is the highest thing in all this (universe).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(स्ता᳘) ता᳘मुत्तमामु᳘पदधात्यमृ᳘तं त᳘दस्य स᳘र्व्वस्योत्तमं᳘ दधाति
</details>

therefore the gods are not separated therefrom; and therefore they are immortal. Thus much as to the deity [^egg_259].

## १७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (3/42 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[अथाध्यात्मं᳓|अ᳓थाध्यात्मं᳓]] यै᳓वेयं᳓ प्रतिष्ठा᳓ य᳓श्चाय᳓म᳓वाङ्प्राण᳓स्त᳓त्प्रथमा᳓ [[चितिर्मांसं᳓|चि᳓तिर्मांसं᳓]] पु᳓रीषं य᳓त्प्रथमां᳓ चि᳓तिं पु᳓रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳓यत्येत᳓दस्य᳓ [[त᳓दात्मनो|त᳓दात्म᳓नो]] मांसैः᳓ सं᳓च्छादयती᳓ष्टका उपधा᳓या᳓स्थी᳓ष्टका अ᳓स्थि त᳓न्मांसैः᳓ सं᳓च्छादयति ना᳙ध᳓स्तात्सं᳓च्छादयति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Now as to the adhyātma (pertaining to the self or body) aspect. The first layer constitutes the legs and the apāna or the downward breath. The loose soil thereon is the flesh. So, when he covers the first layer with loose soil, he covers that (lower) part of the body with flesh. (He does so) after laying down the bricks. Bricks are the bones. Thereby he covers the bones with flesh. He does not cover the bottom (of the first layer).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अथाध्यात्मं यै᳘वेयं᳘ प्रतिष्ठा य᳘श्चायम᳘वाङ्प्राणस्त᳘त्प्रथमा चि᳘ति᳘र्माꣳसं पु᳘रीषं य᳘त्प्रथमां चि᳘तिं पु᳘रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳘यत्येत᳘दस्य त᳘दात्मनो माꣳसैः सं᳘च्छादयती᳘ष्टका उपधायास्थी᳘ष्टका अ᳘स्थि त᳘न्माꣳसैः सं᳘च्छादयति᳘ नाध᳘स्तात्सं᳘च्छादयति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/27 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

एत᳓दस्य᳓ त᳓दात्म᳓न उप᳓रिष्टान्मांसैः᳓ सं᳓च्छादयति [[त᳓स्मादस्त्यैतदात्मन|त᳓स्मादस्त्यैत᳓दात्म᳓न]] उप᳓रिष्टान्मांसैः᳓ सं᳓च्छादयति त᳓स्मादस्त्यैत᳓दात्म᳓न उप᳓रिष्टान्मांसैः᳓ सं᳓च्छन्नं᳓ ना᳓वकाशते ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

But he covers the surface (of the layer). Thereby he covers the upper parts of the body with flesh and hence that part of the body (of the altar), being covered with flesh is not exposed.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एत᳘दस्य त᳘दात्म᳘न उप᳘रिष्टान्माꣳसैः सं᳘च्छादयति त᳘स्मादस्त्यैतदात्मन उप᳘रिष्टान्माꣳसैः सं᳘च्छादयति त᳘स्मादस्त्यैत᳘दात्म᳘न उप᳘रिष्टान्माꣳसैः सं᳘च्छन्नं ना᳘वकाशते ॥१७॥
</details>

## १८

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

य᳓दूर्ध्वं᳓ प्रतिष्ठा᳓या अवाची᳓नं म᳓ध्यात्त᳓द्द्विती᳓या चि᳓तिर्मांसं᳓ पु᳓रीषं य᳓द्विती᳓यां चि᳓तिं पु᳓रीषेण प्रच्छाद᳓यत्येत᳓दस्य᳓ त᳓दात्म᳓नो मांसैः᳓ सं᳓च्छादयती᳓ष्टका उपधा᳓या᳓स्थी᳓ष्टका अ᳓स्थि त᳓न्मांसैः᳓ सं᳓च्छादयति पु᳓रीष उ᳓पदधाति पु᳓रीषेण प्र᳓च्छादयति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The second layer constitutes that part of his (altar's) body which is above the legs and below the waist and the loose soil that is filled is flesh. When he fills the second layer with loose soil, he covers his body with flesh. He covers so, after placing the bricks because bricks are bones, which he covers with flesh.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

## १९

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/27 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[म᳓ध्यमेन|मध्यमे᳓न]] तृती᳓या चि᳓तिर्य᳓दूर्ध्वं᳓ म᳓ध्यादवाची᳓नं ग्रीवा᳓भ्यस्त᳓च्चतुर्थी᳓ चि᳓तिर्ग्रीवा᳓ एव᳓ पञ्चमी᳓ चि᳓तिः शि᳓र एव᳓ षष्ठी᳓ चि᳓तिः प्राणा᳓ एव᳓ सप्तमी᳓ [[चि᳓तिस्ता᳓मुत्तमामुपदधाति᳓|चि᳓तिस्ता᳓मुत्तमा᳓मुपदधाति᳓]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

This third layer is the middle (part) itself. That which is above the waist and below the neck is the fourth layer. The neck is the fifth layer. The head forms the sixth layer. The prāṇas are the seventh layer.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

म᳘ध्यमेन᳘ तृती᳘या चि᳘तिर्य᳘दूर्ध्वं म᳘ध्यादवाची᳘नं ग्रीवा᳘भ्यस्त᳘च्चतुर्थी चि᳘तिर्ग्रीवा᳘ एव᳘ पञ्चमी चि᳘तिः शि᳘र एव᳘ षष्ठी चि᳘तिः प्राणा᳘ एव᳘ सप्तमी चि᳘तिस्ता᳘मुत्तमामुपदधाति
</details>

and the filling of soil means flesh; he thus covers (the channels of) the vital airs with flesh.

Loose soil is flesh. By means of flesh he establishes those prāṇas. He does not cover them above.

<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

He does not cover it above, whence these (channels of the) vital airs are not closed up above.

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Therefore, these prāṇas are uncovered above. (Fourth Brāhmaṇa Ends.) (Chapter Two Ends.)
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

**इत्याहवनीयाग्निचयनं समाप्तम् ।**
</details>
